00263: def descendant_meshes(marker):
00264:     found = []
00265:     pending = list(marker.children)
00266:     while pending:
00267:         obj = pending.pop()
00268:         if obj.type == "MESH":
00269:             found.append(obj)
00270:         pending.extend(obj.children)
00271:     return found
00272: 
00273: for i in range(len(lamp_locations)):
00274:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(f"pendant_lamp_{i}")
00275:     assert marker is not None
00276:     meshes = descendant_meshes(marker)
00277:     assert meshes
00278:     for obj in meshes:
00279:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00280:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00281:             mat = slot.material
00282:             if mat is None:
00283:                 continue
00284:             if min(mat.diffuse_color[:3]) > .7:
00285:                 local = mat.copy()
00286:                 slot.material = local
00287:                 local.use_nodes = True
00288:                 bsdf = local.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00289:                 if bsdf:

00253: def descendant_meshes(marker):
00254:     found=[]
00255:     pending=list(marker.children)
00256:     while pending:
00257:         obj=pending.pop()
00258:         if obj.type=="MESH":
00259:             found.append(obj)
00260:         pending.extend(obj.children)
00261:     return found

00263: def descendant_meshes(marker):
00264:     found = []
00265:     pending = list(marker.children)
00266:     while pending:
00267:         obj = pending.pop()
00268:         if obj.type == "MESH":
00269:             found.append(obj)
00270:         pending.extend(obj.children)
00271:     return found

00059: def B(owner, slot, size, center, color):
00060:     return owner.include(slot, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00061: 
00062: def R(owner, slot, radius, start, end, color):
00063:     return owner.include(slot, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00064: 
00065: def E(owner, slot, center, radii, color):
00066:     node = ball(1, center=center, color=color)
00067:     node.resize(radii, pivot=center)
00068:     return owner.include(slot, node)
00069: 
00070: def tube(owner, slot, points, radius, color):
00071:     return owner.include(slot, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00072: 
00073: def ring(owner, slot, center, rx, rz, radius, color, count=28):
00074:     x, y, z = center
00075:     points = [(x + rx*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/count), y,
00076:                z + rz*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/count)) for i in range(count+1)]
00077:     return tube(owner, slot, points, radius, color)

00237: # Inline pre-execution self-review:
00238: # Inventory, complete owners, dimensions, support and transforms preserved.
00239: # Counter-supported bodies use z=.90; fascia/backsplash are not support anchors.
00240: # About 250 expanded primitives; 35 slats and 28-segment ring are main repeats.
00241: # Tubes expand to rods/balls; no dense textile or grain geometry is introduced.
00242: # Positive finite dimensions; no fillet radii; adjacent tube endpoints distinct.
00243: # Every repeated include slot is indexed and unique within its parent.
00244: # All geometry remains in SceneAct; realize precedes camera/world/lights.
00245: # Counter material changes use exact nonempty component descendants and copies.
00246: # Other material edits are explicit palette choices in their original builders.
00247: # Shader code preserves owner transforms and mesh topology.
00248: # Review is not a claim of collision accuracy or passing regression tests.
00249: realize(scene)
00250: 
00251: def meshes_under(slot):
00252:     obj=bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00253:     assert obj is not None, slot
00254:     meshes=[x for x in obj.children_recursive if x.type=='MESH']
00255:     if obj.type=='MESH':
00256:         meshes.append(obj)
00257:     assert meshes, slot
00258:     return meshes
00259: 
00260: # Procedural granite is restricted to the three counter components.
00261: # No input image or external texture is used.
00262: for component in ('counter_slab', 'backsplash', 'counter_front'):
00263:     for obj in meshes_under(component):
00264:         obj.data=obj.data.copy()
00265:         for index,old in enumerate(list(obj.data.materials)):
00266:             if old is None:
00267:                 continue
00268:             mat=old.copy()
00269:             obj.data.materials[index]=mat
00270:             mat.use_nodes=True
00271:             nt=mat.node_tree
00272:             bs=nt.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00273:             assert bs is not None
00274:             tex=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00275:             noise=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00276:             noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=175
00277:             noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00278:             ramp=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00279:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.28
00280:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=(.025,.021,.013,1)
00281:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.72
00282:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=(.265,.225,.15,1)
00283:             middle=ramp.color_ramp.elements.new(.50)
00284:             middle.color=(.105,.085,.052,1)
00285:             nt.links.new(tex.outputs['Object'],noise.inputs['Vector'])
00286:             nt.links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],ramp.inputs['Fac'])
00287:             nt.links.new(ramp.outputs['Color'],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00288:             bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.72
